    v = v_raw.copy()
    v[-1] = 0.0  # but if using x length n-1, cannot modify x? It's local.
    p = softmax(v)
    b = conv_self_fft(p, m)
    # stable logsumexp
    bm = b.max()
    e = np.exp((b-bm)/tau)
    H = bm/tau + np.log(e.sum())

 ∂(r_i/D)/∂x_a = (δ_{ia} r_a D - r_i ∂D/∂x_a)/D^2 = p_i(δ_{ia} - p_a) because ∂D/∂x_a=r_a, so r_i r_a/D^2 = p_i p_a. Thus ∂p_i/∂x_a = p_i(δ_{ia}-p_a). Including last: p_last(0 - p_a) = -p_last p_a. This is the same as full softmax with fixed base last=0 (not p_last in derivative). So gradient in x: grad_x_a = Σ_i g_i p_i(δ_{ia}-p_a) = p_a(g_a - Σ_i g_i p_i) = p_a(g_a - dot(p,g)). Interesting! For p_last derivative doesn't matter because formula same except a not last. Let's test with n=2: x=[x0], p0=e^x/(1+e^x), p1=1/(1+e^x)=p_last. grad_x0 = p0(g0 - dot). Dot=p0g0+p1g1. =p0g0 -p0p0g0 -p0p1g1. From direct: g0 ∂p0 + g1 ∂p1 = g0 p0(1-p0) + g1(-p0p1)=p0g0 -p0^2g0 -p0p1g1 correct. Good. So formula is simple: `gx = p[:n-1] * (g[:n-1] - dot)`, where dot = p@g. This is true gradient with last fixed. Great. It does not include g_last except via dot. This removes shift. Use this.

Now LP refine details. Implement `_active_lp_update(a, deadline, max_iters, K)`. Return best. Pseudocode:
```
def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, max_iters=50):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0); best=a; best_score=score(a)
    L=2*n-1
    A_structure? dense building each iter.
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > deadline: break
       b=conv_self_fft(a, m); M=b.max(); # score=2nM (a sum1)
       if M<=0: break
       # active selection
       # threshold select = max(0.98*M, M - 0.02* (b.max()-b.min())?)
       # To ensure at least 64 constraints.
       thresh = 0.985*M
       idx = np.where(b >= thresh)[0]
       if len(idx) < 64:
           idx = np.argpartition(b, -64)[-64:]
       # cap K=1200? sort by b desc maybe top K.
       maxK = 1200 if n<=4096 else 2048
       if len(idx)>maxK:
           idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[-maxK:]]
       # Build A dense K x n
       A = np.zeros((len(idx), n))
       for r,k in enumerate(idx):
           k0=max(0, k-n+1); k1=min(n-1,k)
           seg = a[k-k0 : k-k1-1 : -1]  # length k1-k0+1
           A[r, k0:k1+1] = seg
       c = np.ones(n)
       # optional upper = max(1.0, 100*max(a))
       U = 1.0
       res = linprog(c=-c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(len(idx), M), bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       if not res.success: continue
       g=res.x; if g is None: continue
       g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum()
       if s<=1e-12: continue
       g/=s
       # line search blend
       trial_best_score=best_score; trial_best=a
       # t candidates: 0, small...1
       ts = [0.05,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.7,1.0]
       # optional use s to choose first
       for t in ts:
           cand = (1-t)*a + t*g; cand=normalize(cand)
           sc=score(cand)
           if sc<trial_best_score:
              trial=...
       # If best t==0 (no improve), maybe try smaller 0.01-0.05
       if trial_best is a:
          for t in np.linspace(0.01,0.05,5):...
       if improved: a=trial; best_score=...
       # If Sg not much >1 or no improve, break
       if s <= 1.001 + 1e-8: break
       if not improved:
          # maybe add noise to current a and continue? break or perturb
          # perturb current best and continue few
          a = normalize(best + rng.normal(0,0.001/n?))
       if time > deadline - 60: break
    return normalize(best),best_score
```
Build A dense with loop; for K=1200,n=2048, loops 1200, each slice assignment length avg n/2, total 1.5M assignments, okay. But repeated 50 times heavy. Limit max_iters to ~15. We can prestructure row lengths? not necessary.

Linprog with dense A K=1200, n=2048, b_ub. It may be slow. Use `method='highs'`. Options possibly `{'time_limit': 20}`? HiGHS supports `time_limit` maybe. We can set `options={'time_limit': 15, 'output_flag': False}`. `linprog` options include `time_limit`. Good. If not, okay. But if time_limit stops, res.success may be False; we can still use x? Maybe not. Use no time limit? To avoid timeout, set max 20s per LP. If 15 LPs, 300s. Use `time_limit=15`.

Important insight: Objectively, small lags k correspond to local correlation of sequence near start. To keep M low, the sequence must start not too large but also not too small? Easier using dense? Sparse from dense `scipy.sparse.csr_matrix(A)` duplicates memory. Instead build directly using blocks: For each j, rows j:j+n, cols j, data an. Could accumulate lists; 4M elements. Do:
```
from scipy import sparse
row_parts=[]; col_parts=[]; data_parts=[]
for j in range(n):
    # rows = j + np.arange(n)
    row_parts.append(np.arange(j, j+n))
    col_parts.append(np.full(n, j))
    data_parts.append(an)
A = sparse.csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data_parts), (np.concatenate(row_parts), np.concatenate(col_parts))), shape=(L,n))
```

def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_seconds=None, full=True, K_cap=4095):

    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    it=0
    no_improve=0

       it+=1

       # if M<=0 break

       # K sequence: first try full or K_cap; if fail, smaller
       g, s = _solve_lp(a,b,M, idx, timeout)
       if g is None:
           # try smaller K
           idx_small = top min(1024,L)
           g,s=...
           if g is None: break
       gn=normalize(g)
       # line search
       (best, best_score, a, improved) = _line_search_blend(a, gn, best, best_score)
       if improved:
           no_improve=0
           # update best maybe a

           no_improve +=1
           # If Sg <=1 break

           # perturb a around best to escape

           # try again a few

       # if time low break
       print
    return best,best_score

def _blend_search(a, g, best_a, best_score):

    # refine around best_t if in (0,1)
    if 0<best_t<1:
       lo=max(0,best_t-0.05); hi=min(1,best_t+0.05)
       for t in np.linspace(lo,hi,15): ...

LP solve helper:
```
def _solve_lp(a,b,M,K=4095,retain=...,timeout=20):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if K>=L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    # sort? not needed
    scale=np.max(a)
    if scale<=0: return None,0
    aset=a/scale
    rhs=M/scale
    A=np.empty((len(idx),n)); A.fill(0.0)
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       k0=max(0,k-n+1); k1=min(n-1,k)
       A[r,k0:k1+1] = aset[k-k0 : k-k1-1 : -1]
    c=-np.ones(n)
    bounds=[(0,100.0)]*n
    res=optimize.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(len(idx), rhs), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': timeout, 'output_flag':False})
    if res.x is not None and np.isfinite(res.x).all():
       g=np.maximum(res.x,0)
       s=float(g.sum())
       # check feasibility? A@ g <= rhs+tol maybe
       if s>0:
          return g,s
    return None,0
```
This builds dense A row-wise for active. For full K=4095, A 4095x2048=67MB. bounds list length 2048. `linprog` may take. To reduce memory for bounds repeatedly, global list? fine.

Let's write helper `_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite: a.fill(1.0/max(1,len(a)))
    else: a/=s
    return a
```

`_interp`:
```
def _interp(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if ...; x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a/s); out=out.maximum(0); ss=out.sum(); return out/ss if ss>0 else ones/n
```

Now, code for `_solve_lp_full`:
```
def _solve_lp_full(a, M, timeout):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    amax=float(a.max())
    if amax <= 0 or M <= 0: return None,0.0
    asc = a/amax
    A = np.empty((L, n), dtype=float)
    A.fill(0.0)
    # column assignment
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j] = asc
    c = -np.ones(n)
    ub = M/amax
    bounds = [(0.0, 100.0)]*n
    res = optimize.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L, ub), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'output_flag': False, 'time_limit': timeout})
    if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
       g=np.maximum(res.x,0.0)
       s=float(g.sum())
       if s > 1e-8:
           return g, s
    return None,0.0
```
Note: A filled zeros then each column has n entries; for j, rows j..j+n-1 valid because L=2n-1. For j=0, end n; j=n-1 end 2n-1. Good.

def _solve_lp_active(a,b,M,K,timeout):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   if K>=L: return _solve_lp_full(...)
   idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
   # filter duplicates? argpartition unique
   idx.sort()
   amax=...
   asc=a/amax; ub=M/amax
   A=np.empty((K,n)); A.fill(0)
   for r,k in enumerate(idx):
      k0=max(0,k-n+1); k1=min(n-1,k); length=k1-k0+1
      A[r,k0:k1+1] = asc[k-k0 : k-k1-1 : -1]
   ...
```
If `K` small (e.g. 512), A memory 1MB. Good.

def _blend_line_search(a,g,best_a,best_score):
    curr_score = _score_fft(a)
    best_b = min(curr_score, best_score)
    best_a_out = a.copy() if curr_score < best_score else best_a
    # if g is None return
    ts = np.array([0.0,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.08,0.1,0.15,0.2,0.25,0.3,0.4,0.5,0.65,0.8,1.0])
    best_t=0.0
    for t in ts:
       if t==0: sc=curr_score; 
       else: cand=(1-t)*a+t*g; cand=_normalize(cand); sc=_score_fft(cand)
       if sc < best_b - 1e-12:
           best_b=sc; best_a_out=cand; best_t=t
       elif t==0 and sc < best_b:
           best_b=sc; best_a_out=a.copy(); best_t=0
    if 0<best_t<1:
       lo=max(0.0,best_t-0.05); hi=min(1.0,best_t+0.05)
       for t in np.linspace(lo, hi, 15): evaluate
    return best_a_out,best_b
```

Now, L-BFGS state. We need to pass state dict to minimize with args. Top-level functions:
```
def _lbfgs_eval(x, state):
    n=state['n']; m=state['m']; tau=state['tau']
    # build p
    xe=np.exp(np.clip(x,-700,700))
    den=1.0+float(xe.sum())
    p=np.empty(n); p[:n-1]=xe/den; p[-1]=1.0/den
    P=np.fft.fft(p,m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(P*P).real[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    # update best
    sc=2.0*n*float(b.max())
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['best_score']:
       state['best_score']=sc; state['best_a']=p.copy()
    # objective logsumexp
    bmax=float(b.max())
    z=(b-bmax)/tau
    z=np.clip(z,-745.0,700.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    ssum=float(e.sum())
    if ssum<=0 or not finite:
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    val=bmax/tau + np.log(ssum)
    q=e/ssum
    Q=np.fft.fft(q,m)
    c=np.fft.ifft(Q*np.conj(P)).real[:n]
    grad_p = (2.0/tau)*c
    dot=float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    gx = p[:n-1]*(grad_p[:n-1]-dot)
    # cleanup
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)): return val, zeros
    gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
    if gmax > 10.0: gx *= 10.0/gmax
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, zeros
    return float(val), gx
```
Note: If `tau` fixed based on current M. If sequence changes and M much smaller, tau too large maybe. We run sequential tau based on initial/best. Good.

Callback:
```
def _lbfgs_callback(xk, state):
    if time.monotonic() >= state['call_deadline'] - 5.0:
        return False
    return True
```
If callback returns False, minimize stops. If older version doesn't accept, it may ignore; maxiter prevents.

`_lbfgs_refine(a0, deadline, budget=None)`:
```
def _lbfgs_refine(a0, deadline, share_budget=None):
    n=len(a0); m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    state={'n':n,'m':m,'call_deadline':deadline,'best_a':a0.copy(),'best_score':_score_fft(a0)}
    p=state['best_a']
    # fracs and shares
    fracs=[0.20,0.10,0.05,0.025,0.012,0.006]
    shares=[0.12,0.14,0.18,0.20,0.18,0.18]
    total_norm=sum(shares)
    for i,frac in enumerate(fracs):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
       now=time.monotonic()
       rem=deadline-now
       if rem <= 10: break
       per_share=rem*shares[i]/total_norm
       per_deadline=min(deadline-5, now+max(30.0, per_share))
       M=state['best_score']/(2*n)
       tau=max(1e-15, frac*M)
       state['tau']=tau; state['call_deadline']=per_deadline
       # init x from state best
       pbest=state['best_a'].copy()
       pbest=np.maximum(pbest,1e-300)
       x=np.log(pbest[:n-1]/pbest[-1])
       x=np.clip(x,-30.0,30.0)
       bounds=[(-30.0,30.0)]*(n-1)
       res=optimize.minimize(_lbfgs_eval, x, args=(state,), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, callback=_lbfgs_callback, options={'maxiter':300, 'maxls':30, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'disp':False})
       # update state time
       # print
    return state['best_a'], state['best_score']
```

`_adam_refine` code:
```
def _adam_refine(a0, deadline, rng):
    n = len(a0)
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = _score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): ...
    v = _log_plus(a)
    v = v - np.mean(v); v = np.clip(v,-25,25)
    m = _next_pow2(2*n-1)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    mu=np.zeros(n); nu=np.zeros(n); t=0
    start=time.monotonic(); max_seconds=max(1.0, deadline-start)
    deadline_safe = start + max_seconds
    last_improve = start
    check_interval = max(2, min(18, 1800 // max(1,n)))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline_safe - 2.0:
       elapsed = max(1e-9, time.monotonic()-start)
       progress = min(1.0, elapsed/max_seconds)
       stuck_limit = min(90.0, 0.35*max_seconds)
       if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_limit:
           sigma=0.10+0.35*(1-progress)
           v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
           v = v - np.mean(v); v=np.clip(v,-25,25)
           last_improve=time.monotonic()
           continue
       a = _soft(v)
       F = np.fft.fft(a,m)
       b = np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
       b = np.maximum(b,0)
       bmax=float(b.max())
       if not finite or <=1e-300: break
       tau_frac=0.18*(1-progress)+0.03
       if rng.random() <0.25: tau_frac *= rng.uniform(0.3,3.0)
       tau=max(1e-15, bmax*max(tau_frac,1e-4))
       x=(b-bmax)/tau; x=np.clip(x,-60,60); x-=x.max(); w=np.exp(x); ws=w.sum(); ...
       blen=b.size; top_k=max(1,int(0.05*blen)); if top_k<blen: idx=np.argpartition(b,-top_k)[-top_k:]; w[idx]+=0.5; ...
       P=np.fft.fft(w,m)
       c=np.fft.ifft(P*np.conj(F)).real[:n]
       np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
       cm=float(np.dot(c,a))
       gv=a*(c-cm)
       grms=np.sqrt(np.mean(gv*gv)); if too small noise continue
       gv/=grms; t+=1; ... Adam step
       lr=0.10*(1-0.65*progress)
       step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps); step=np.clip(step,-0.5,0.5)
       v -= lr*step; v-=mean; clip
       if t % check_interval ==0:
          an=_soft(v); Fn=np.fft.fft(an,m); sc=_score_from_F(an,Fn,m)
          if sc<best_score-1e-9: update
    # final update
    return _normalize(best_a), best_score
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy import optimize

"""
Search for nonnegative step-function coefficients minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The main idea is to use the LP directional scheme from the known state of the art:
for a normalized current vector a and M = max(a*a), solve

    max sum(g)  s.t.  (a*g)_k <= M, g >= 0,

then rescale g and blend it into a with a line search.  This is repeated as a
successive linear program.  To escape local flat spots, the code also performs
smooth log-sum-exp L-BFGS refinement in softmax/log space and a projected
Adam-style gradient step.  The best sequence found before the deadline is
returned.
"""

class _SearchTimeout(Exception):
    pass


_LBFGS_STATE = {}


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a.fill(1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    else:
        a /= s
    return a


def _interp(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / float(int(n))
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == n:
        out = a / s
        return out / float(out.sum()) if float(out.sum()) > 0 else (np.ones(n) / n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a / s)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / ss


def _conv_self_fft(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * a.size - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    n = int(a.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    b = _conv_self_fft(a, m)
    bm = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * bm / (s * s))


def _score_from_F(a, F, m):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    bm = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * bm / (s * s))


def _soft(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _log_plus(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    return np.log(a)


def _solve_lp_full(a, M, timeout):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if amax <= 1e-300 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None, 0.0

    asc = a / amax
    A = np.empty((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A.fill(0.0)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = asc

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ub = float(M / amax)
    ub = max(1e-12, ub * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12)

    try:
        res = optimize.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(L, ub, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=[(0.0, 10.0)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"output_flag": False, "time_limit": max(1.0, float(timeout))}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, 0.0

    x = res.x
    if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None, 0.0

    g = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(gs):
        return None, 0.0
    return g, gs


def _solve_lp_active(a, b, M, K, timeout):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if K >= L:
        return _solve_lp_full(a, M, timeout)
    K = int(max(32, min(K, L)))

    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if amax <= 1e-300 or M <= 1e-300:
        return None, 0.0

    idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    idx.sort()

    asc = a / amax
    A = np.empty((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    A.fill(0.0)

    for r, k in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(k)
        k0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        k1 = min(n - 1, k)
        starta = k - k1
        enda = k - k0 + 1
        A[r, k0:k1 + 1] = asc[starta:enda][::-1]

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ub = float(M / amax)
    ub = max(1e-12, ub * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12)

    try:
        res = optimize.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(K, ub, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=[(0.0, 10.0)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"output_flag": False, "time_limit": max(1.0, float(timeout))}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, 0.0

    x = res.x
    if x is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None, 0.0

    g = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(gs):
        return None, 0.0
    return g, gs


def _blend_line_search(a, g, best_a, best_score):
    a = _normalize(a)
    g = _normalize(g)

    curr = _score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(curr):
        curr = np.inf

    out_score = min(curr, best_score)
    out_a = a.copy() if curr < best_score else best_a
    best_t = 0.0

    ts = np.array([0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.05, 0.08, 0.10, 0.15,
                   0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50, 0.65, 0.80, 1.00], dtype=np.float64)

    for t in ts:
        t = float(t)
        if t <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score_fft(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < out_score - 1e-12:
            out_score = sc
            out_a = cand
            best_t = t

    if 0.0 < best_t < 1.0:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.05)
        hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.05)
        ts2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 17, dtype=np.float64)
        for t in ts2:
            cand = (1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * g
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            sc = _score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < out_score - 1e-12:
                out_score = sc
                out_a = cand

    return out_a, out_score


def _lp_refine(a0, deadline, rng, max_iters=200):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_a, np.inf

    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    full_allowed = (n <= 2048)
    full_fail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    active_Ks = [int(max(1024, 0.75 * L)), 1024, 512]

    while time.monotonic() < max(deadline, time.monotonic()) - 8.0 and it < max_iters:
        it += 1

        a = _normalize(a)
        b = _conv_self_fft(a, m)
        M = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        sc = 2.0 * n * M
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        g = None
        s = 0.0

        if full_allowed:
            timeout = 30.0 if it <= 5 else 20.0
            g, s = _solve_lp_full(a, M, timeout)
            if g is None:
                full_fail += 1
                if full_fail >= 3:
                    full_allowed = False
            else:
                full_fail = 0

        if g is None:
            for K in active_Ks:
                K = int(max(64, min(K, L)))
                if g is None:
                    g, s = _solve_lp_active(a, b, M, K, 15.0)
                if g is not None:
                    break

        if g is None or s <= 1e-12:
            break

        g = _normalize(g)
        new_a, new_score = _blend_line_search(a, g, best_a, best_score)

        if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_a = new_a
            best_score = new_score
            a = best_a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            print("LP it %d n=%d score=%.9f s_g=%.5f" % (it, n, best_score, s))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if s <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
                break

            sigma = 0.012 / np.sqrt(max(1, n)) * (1.0 + 0.25 * no_improve)
            a = _normalize(best_a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))

            if no_improve >= 5:
                break

    return _normalize(best_a), float(best_score)


def _lbfgs_eval(x, state):
    if time.monotonic() > state.get("call_deadline", 0.0):
        raise _SearchTimeout()

    n = int(state["n"])
    m = int(state["m"])
    tau = float(state["tau"])

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0)

    xe = np.exp(x)
    den = 1.0 + float(xe.sum())
    if den <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(den):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:n - 1] = xe / den
    p[-1] = 1.0 / den

    P = np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(P * P).real[:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

    bm = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    sc = 2.0 * n * bm
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state["best_score"]:
        state["best_score"] = float(sc)
        state["best_a"] = p.copy()

    z = (b - bm) / tau
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    esum = float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(esum):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    val = fm / tau + np.log(esum)

    q = e / esum
    Q = np.fft.fft(q, m)
    c = np.fft.ifft(Q * np.conj(P)).real[:n]

    gp = (2.0 / tau) * c
    dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    gx = p[:n - 1] * (gp[:n - 1] - dot)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
    if gmax > 10.0:
        gx *= 10.0 / gmax

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), gx


def _lbfgs_callback(xk):
    state = _LBFGS_STATE
    try:
        dl = float(state.get("call_deadline", 0.0))
    except Exception:
        return True
    if time.monotonic() >= dl - 5.0:
        return False
    return True


def _lbfgs_refine(a0, deadline):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    state = {
        "n": n,
        "m": m,
        "call_deadline": deadline,
        "best_a": a0.copy(),
        "best_score": _score_fft(a0),
        "tau": 1e-3
    }

    global _LBFGS_STATE
    _LBFGS_STATE = state

    fracs = [0.18, 0.10, 0.055, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008]
    shares = [0.12, 0.14, 0.18, 0.20, 0.18, 0.18]
    total_share = float(sum(shares))

    for i, frac in enumerate(fracs):
        if time.monotonic() >= max(deadline, time.monotonic()) - 12.0:
            break

        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - now)
        if rem <= 12.0:
            break

        per = rem * (shares[i] / total_share)
        call_deadline = min(float(deadline) - 5.0, now + max(30.0, per))

        M = state["best_score"] / (2.0 * n)
        tau = max(1e-15, frac * max(M, 1e-15))
        state["tau"] = tau
        state["call_deadline"] = call_deadline

        pbest = state["best_a"]
        pbest = np.maximum(np.asarray(pbest, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
        ss = float(pbest.sum())
        if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
            pbest = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            pbest = pbest / ss

        den = max(float(pbest[-1]), 1e-100)
        x = np.log(pbest[:n - 1] / (den + 1e-300))
        x = np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0)

        bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * (n - 1)

        try:
            optimize.minimize(
                _lbfgs_eval,
                x,
                args=(state,),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=_lbfgs_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 220,
                    "maxls": 30,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "disp": False
                }
            )
        except _SearchTimeout:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Synchronize state best back into local best.
        if "best_a" in state and np.isfinite(state["best_score"]):
            a0 = _normalize(state["best_a"])

    return _normalize(state["best_a"]), float(state["best_score"])


def _adam_refine(a0, deadline, rng):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(a0)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return best_a, np.inf

    v = _log_plus(a0)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -25.0, 25.0)

    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    max_seconds = max(1.0, float(deadline) - start)
    last_improve = start
    check_interval = max(2, min(18, 1800 // max(1, n)))

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 2.0:
        elapsed = max(1e-9, time.monotonic() - start)
        progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max_seconds)

        stuck_limit = min(90.0, 0.35 * max_seconds)
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            sigma = 0.10 + 0.35 * (1.0 - progress)
            v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -25.0, 25.0)
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            continue

        a = _soft(v)
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        bmax = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        tau_frac = 0.18 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.03
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            tau_frac *= float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
        tau = max(1e-15, bmax * max(tau_frac, 1e-4))

        x = (b - bmax) / tau
        x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
        x = x - float(x.max())
        w = np.exp(x)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws

        blen = int(b.size)
        top_k = max(1, int(0.05 * blen))
        if top_k < blen:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -top_k)[-top_k:]
        else:
            idx = np.arange(blen)
        w[idx] += 0.5
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws

        P = np.fft.fft(w, m)
        c = np.fft.ifft(P * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

        cm = float(np.dot(c, a))
        gv = a * (c - cm)
        grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv * gv)))
        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-14:
            sigma = 0.05 + 0.20 * (1.0 - progress)
            v += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -25.0, 25.0)
            continue

        gv = gv / grms
        t += 1
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gv
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gv * gv)

        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        denom1 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        denom2 = max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        mhat = mu / denom1
        vhat = nu / denom2

        lr = 0.10 * (1.0 - 0.65 * progress)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -0.5, 0.5)

        v = v - lr * step
        v = v - float(np.mean(v))
        v = np.clip(v, -25.0, 25.0)

        if t % check_interval == 0:
            an = _soft(v)
            Fn = np.fft.fft(an, m)
            sc = _score_from_F(an, Fn, m)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = an.copy()
                last_improve = time.monotonic()

    an = _soft(v)
    sc = _score_fft(an)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_a = an

    return _normalize(best_a), float(best_score)


def _maybe_validate(a):
    best = np.inf
    try:
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
            arr = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(arr))
            if np.isfinite(val):
                best = val
    except Exception:
        best = np.inf
    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(120.0, budget_s))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(120.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = 2048

    prev = None
    try:
        hs = height_sequence_1
        if isinstance(hs, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                    prev = _interp(arr, n)
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    sqrt_start = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) ** (-0.5)
    sqrt_start = _normalize(sqrt_start)

    if prev is not None:
        best_a = _interp(prev, n)
        best_score = _score_fft(best_a)
    else:
        best_a = sqrt_start.copy()
        best_score = _score_fft(best_a)

    print("Initial best score: %.9f" % best_score)

    total = max(120.0, deadline - t0)

    # Short exploratory LP from the previous construction and a one-sided
    # square-root start.  If the previous sequence is unavailable, skip the
    # extra start to keep the timeline simple.
    if total > 700.0 and prev is not None:
        d1 = t0 + max(60.0, 0.04 * total)
        a1, s1 = _lp_refine(best_a, d1, rng, max_iters=5)
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_a, best_score = a1, s1

        d2 = t0 + max(100.0, 0.08 * total)
        a2, s2 = _lp_refine(sqrt_start, d2, rng, max_iters=4)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_a, best_score = a2, s2

        print("After short LP best: %.9f" % best_score)

    # Main successive LP phase.
    d_lp1 = t0 + max(200.0, 0.55 * total)
    best_a, best_score = _lp_refine(best_a, d_lp1, rng, max_iters=300)
    print("After main LP best: %.9f" % best_score)

    # Smooth log-space refinement.
    d_lbfgs = t0 + max(300.0, 0.78 * total)
    best_a, best_score = _lbfgs_refine(best_a, d_lbfgs)
    print("After L-BFGS best: %.9f" % best_score)

    # Another LP pass to exploit the smooth improvement.
    d_lp2 = min(deadline - 45.0, t0 + max(400.0, 0.90 * total))
    best_a, best_score = _lp_refine(best_a, d_lp2, rng, max_iters=80)
    print("After second LP best: %.9f" % best_score)

    # Final projected-gradient polish.
    d_adam = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 15.0)
    best_a, best_score = _adam_refine(best_a, d_adam, rng)
    print("After Adam best: %.9f" % best_score)

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)

    # For moderate lengths, verify against the official evaluator when available.
    exact = _maybe_validate(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(exact):
        print("Final FFT score=%.9f exact score=%.9f n=%d" % (best_score, exact, best_a.size))
    else:
        print("Final FFT score=%.9f n=%d" % (best_score, best_a.size))

    # Safety fallback.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)) or best_a.size == 0:
        best_a = _normalize(sqrt_start)

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
